## Self-review

**Spec coverage.** §11 task 12 — the three areas spec §4.3 names (Task 6), and R1's extension to the memory surface (Tasks 3, 4, 5, 7) and R2's `set_props_many` (Task 8). Task 13 — `min` (Task 2); "move the listing" recorded as done (S2) and verified (Task 16 Step 4). Task 14 — README restructure and install paths (Task 13). Task 15 — `quickstart.md:83`, `design.md`, unpublishing (Task 14); the UI page and the rendered site are **not** covered, by PD-5 and R5's scope, and are listed under "Not in this plan". Task 16 — scale restated (Task 12); the table (Task 11, as PD-2 rules). Task 18 — Tasks 15 and 16, stopping before the tag. §5.1's "loudest line" (Task 15 Step 1). §7 — S3, and the two blind spots (Task 16 Step 2). §8.4 — done by plan 1; the census's weakness is ledger row 46. §8.5 — the listing re-measured after the `min` description changed (Task 2 Step 10, Task 16 Step 4). §10's cut line — the two tables under "Scope note". §12 — row 63, PD-3, Task 14 Step 3.

**Controller rulings.** PD-1 → Task 2. PD-2 → Task 11. PD-3 → no task; recorded. PD-4 → Task 12. PD-5 → not in this plan. PD-6 → Task 16. R1 → Tasks 3–7, less `analyze` (DV-1). R2 → Task 8. R3 → Task 15, less its parked clause — the amendments rewrote that changelog text, so the controller dropped the clause and Task 15 Step 4 verifies the section against the code instead. R4 → Tasks 1, 9, 10; row 43 (fixed by plan 3's second amendment) and row 44 (0.8). R5 → Tasks 13 and 14. R6 → Procedure P, with DV-2's figure. R7 → Global Constraints.

**Placeholder scan.** Four kinds, each deliberate and each a fact that does not exist until a step runs: Task 10 Step 6's and Task 15 Step 3's hook-latency figures; Task 11 Step 10's six benchmark cells; Task 11 Step 3's count of unlistable names; Task 16 Step 7's head commit, test line and rehearsal form. Every other value — thresholds, caps, message strings, byte counts, expected failures — is written out. Three steps tell the executor to copy text rather than print it here, and say why: Task 3 Step 3 and Task 5 Step 3 move existing code and comments ("do not retype"), and Task 6 Step 7 / Task 7 Step 6 take stub docstrings from the Rust comments shown in full in the step before.

**Type consistency.** `FIND_SIMILAR_DEFAULT_MIN` is defined once (Task 2) and read by three surfaces and three tests. `ForgetTarget` / `ForgetReport` / `forget` (Task 3) are used with the same shapes by the server (Task 3 Step 4), the binding (Task 7) and the refusal change (Task 9). `predicate_fields` is defined in `memory::forget` and imported by `memory::suggest`. `Suggestion` and `FilteredSuggestions` (Task 4) are the server's `SuggestOut.suggestions` element and the binding's `suggest_rules` return. `UpsertOutcome` carries `same_as_lost` because `ae4d6c6` added it to the tool's reply. `RecallRows { indexed, terms, hits }` (Task 5) is what Task 7's `recall` returns and what its first test compares against, key for key. `refuse_read_if_scoped`, `json_to_py` and `report_to_py` are defined in Task 6 and used in Task 7. `set_props_many` returns `{"nodes", "props_set", "props_removed"}` in the code, the stub, the README and all eight tests. Ledger rows: entered 38–63 in Task 1 (row 37 is the amendment's and is not renumbered); flipped by Task 9 (39–42), Task 10 (38), Task 14 (60), Task 16 (58, 59); the gate's expected counts are 63 defects, 38 closed, 25 deferred after Task 1, and 42 closed, 21 deferred after Task 9.

**Review-focus check.** RF-1 → `test_a_whole_store_read_is_refused_on_a_scoped_handle` (eight rows in Task 6, three more in Task 7) and the keyed-write rows (Tasks 7, 8). RF-2 → `test_an_unknown_key_is_an_error_even_when_its_row_changes_nothing` (Task 8). RF-3 → `test_rules_fire_in_the_same_commit_and_cost_one_marker_frame` (Task 8). RF-4 → the `mid` fixture in all three `min` tests (Task 2). RF-5 → the "glued" and "path" cases of `test-check-names.sh` (Task 11).

**Where this plan departs from its brief, and why.**

1. **Sixteen tasks, not thirteen.** The relocations are three tasks, not one — `forget`; the suggestion filter with the upsert; `recall` as rows — so each is a reviewable move with its own pins. The carries are two: four code fixes (Task 9) and a measurement that needs a release build and a pre-registered procedure (Task 10).
2. **`analyze`'s shaping is not moved** (DV-1).
3. **`recall` as rows needed a `core-api` change the brief did not list** (Task 5): there was no structured recall to wrap.
4. **The disk threshold is set against 48 GiB, not 9** (DV-2).
5. **The UI page and three other "missing pages" are not written** — spec task 15 names a UI page; R5 does not, and PD-5 moved the site they would live on.
6. **Task 13 fixes two stale facts in the skill** (`about` keys need not exist; `query` takes `--role`). The brief asked only to stay inside the byte cap; both edits shorten it.
7. **Task 11 re-measures the front page's numbers** with the single-engine harness. PD-2 asked for a table whose cells are reproducible from a committed result; the cheapest honest way to make them current is one 10k run. It is a step that can be cut, with a dated fallback.
8. **Task 1 fixes one line of the ledger gate** (it ran only from the repository root) — found reading it, shown failing, one line.
9. **The names list is built from the README table, three tracked files and the gitignored landscape notes under `.superpowers/research/`**, by the controller's ruling; the executor does not ask the owner, and reports the list as possibly incomplete.

**Revised against a pre-flight scan, 2026-10-01.** The scan read this document against the tree and ran three of its scripts from scratch copies. It found thirteen conflicts, six shape errors, eight rubric defects and locator errors in seven tasks; every ruling was applied. The ones that changed what an executor writes: plan 4's ledger rows are 38–63, because row 37 was taken by the amendment; the names gate prints a masked path, because a path that is a name is the name; Task 3 moves the amended `forget` — `aliases_rewritten`, and a batch that rewrites `aliases` with the removal; the listing's expected bytes are 27,067 and 27,868; the Python deltas are +49, +31 and +11, because two parametrised tests read `_DOCUMENTED`, not three; Task 12 spells out "ten million" in a claim file its own pattern scans; Task 13 moves the install section as well as the memory one; every chained build, test and gate runs under `set -o pipefail` or unpiped; and the publish rehearsal stops at a version mismatch instead of building a wheel on it.

**Verified, and how.** Three layers, and which is which matters. *Re-read at `ae4d6c6` for this revision:* `tool_forget`, `ForgetReport` and `render_forget` in full; `tool_upsert_entity`'s body; the public surface of `memory/identity.rs` and `identity_props_after_forgetting_name` in full; `remember.rs`'s imports and anchors; every item and test location cited in `crates/server/src/mcp_tasks.rs`, `crates/server/src/mcp.rs` and `crates/server/tests/mcp.rs`; `run_brief` and `reach_line`; the ledger's markers, row 37 and length; the changelog's headings and listing bytes; the spec's headings and correction labels; and the output of `check-defect-ledger.sh` (37 / 35 / 2) and `ignored-test-census.sh` (32 / 9 / 23). *Unchanged since `f198839` by `git diff --stat f198839..ae4d6c6`, so carried:* the binding, `http.rs`, `db.rs`, `algo.rs`, `recall.rs`, `memory/schema.rs`, `memory/brief.rs`, `explain_digest.rs`, every script, every workflow, `README.md`, `llms.txt` and every `docs/site` page but `mcp.md` (whose five cited locations were re-checked). *Checked by the pre-flight scan, not by this author:* about two hundred cites at `c8c8057`, and three of this plan's scripts run from scratch copies — the names gate and its test (which found the path defect now fixed), the link gate (one `ANCHOR` and fourteen `UNINDEXED`, as predicted) and the generator's `--check`. Originally read at `f198839`, by this plan's author or by two read-only verification passes whose quotes were then used: every `file:line` in the tasks; the full text of `tool_forget`, `tool_suggest_rules`, `tool_upsert_entity`, `tool_remember`, `rule_owned_refusal`, `recall_digest`, `render_schema`, `render_explain`, `describe_entity_with_aliases`, `upsert_node`, `find_similar` (all three surfaces) and the binding's access helpers and converters; the signatures listed in Task 8 Step 3; the four algorithm config structs' field lists; `RuleDef`'s fields and serde attributes; `scripts/check-claims.sh`, `gen-llms-full.sh`, `bump-version.sh`, `check-defect-ledger.sh` and all three workflows in full; `README.md` and `CHANGELOG.md`'s 0.7.0 section in full. Run, read-only: `claude plugin marketplace --help` (the subcommand exists; `claude marketplace` does not); `cargo metadata` (the ten packages and which are unpublishable); `bump-version.sh --check`, `check-defect-ledger.sh`, `check-claims.sh`, `ignored-test-census.sh` (all green at `4141c4e`); `cargo publish --help` and `cargo package --help` (the flags Task 16 uses exist on 1.92.0). Computed: the `min` description's byte change (255 → 185, so 27,137 → 27,067 and 27,938 → 27,868); the four vocabulary words Task 10 uses.

**Not verified, and named where used.**

- **Nothing was compiled or run** — no Rust test, no pytest, no script written here. Every "Expected" line is a prediction from reading.
- **The Rust baseline** was not measured by this plan; it uses the controller's figure for `ae4d6c6` and deltas, and Task 1 Step 1 re-measures.
- **The Python baseline** does not exist until Procedure P's first run.
- **Engine behaviours the new tests assert, read but not run:** that `delete_rule` retracts its edges (Task 6); that a fresh stub carries `provisional: True` and the exact alias list (Task 7); that `brief.nodes == 2` (Task 7); the frame deltas of `set_props_many` — probed on the 0.6.11 wheel for `upsert_node` and `ingest_batch`, not for a `set_prop` batch on this tree (Task 8); that `GraphDb::explain` returns one entry per direction with `src_key`/`dst_key` as stored (Task 9); that the direct-call store in `memory_suggest.rs` draws the same proposals as the server's fixture (Task 4); that twelve entities are enough for `suggest_rules` to propose over `team` through `remember` (Task 13 — the step is written to handle either outcome).
- **Whether the engine accepts a 703-character label** — Task 9's schema-cap test needs twenty of them. If it refuses, give the nodes a short label and twenty long **property names** instead; the cap is on bytes rendered, whichever name is long.
- **The revised `check-names.py`** — the masked-path change was written after the pre-flight ran the original; the pre-flight's run is what found the defect, not what proved the fix.
- **`git add -u -- <dir>` staging nothing untracked** in Task 11 Step 9 is how `git` documents `-u`; not run here.
- **pyo3 0.26 spellings** in `json_to_py` (`py.None().into_bound(py)`) follow the idioms `value_to_py` uses; not compiled.
- **`cargo publish --dry-run --workspace` resolving unpublished siblings** on 1.92 (Task 16 Step 5; a fallback is written).
- **The names worklist** is from a looser search than the gate's; the gate's own output is the authority (Task 11 Step 4). The gitignored landscape notes the executor builds the list from were not read for this document, so how complete they are is unknown.
- **Two doc sentences left alone because neither was checked:** `docs/site/skill.md:296` and `packaging/plugin/README.md:12`.
- **Whether GitHub Pages is enabled** (PD-5).
- **`benchmarks/README.md`'s workload table** is written from `run.py`'s report code and `test_harness.py`'s imports, not from `adapters/ours.py` line by line; Task 11 Step 7 says to check it.
